Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on condensed_matter structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 420. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 927 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on condensed_matter structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 420. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 927. QED.
